Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the computational structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 221. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 826 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to computational theory, we decompose the problem into 221 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 826. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
